[[sep-rom-vplan-fuse-lock]]
===== Fuse-secret read locks

*Tests:* 4.

*Base:* `hw/sys/sep/dv/cocotb/tests/rom_fw/sep_fuse_lock_base.py`.

*Objective:* Across the 2x2 of lifecycle (TEST_DEV/PROD) and SBOOT_DIS (0/1), the ROM takes the expected secure-boot decision, boots BL1 and sets all six fuse-secret read locks before hand-off.

*Procedure:*

. Stage the cell's +sep_efuse_preload and assert LC raw, SBOOT_DIS, LOCKS=0, LOCKS_SPARE=0 and clean key fuses.
. Pick the signed image when SBOOT_DIS=0 or the unsigned image when SBOOT_DIS=1, and assert each slot's enforced bit matches, the payload is plaintext and no usage constraint is set.
. Plant a lifecycle constraint permitting only the cell's LC on both slots (re-sealed for signed, re-hashed for unsigned).
. Start the ESRC noise driver, boot from SPI and poll cold_scratch[0] for the BL1 PASS verdict.
. Assert required markers: LC_STATE=0x..., LC=..., FUSE: SBOOT_DIS: n, crypto markers or SBOOT_OFF, MANIFEST_OK, FUSE_SECRETS_LOCKED, BL1_COPIED, BL1_JUMP=, BL1 FUSE_CHK, LOCKS=0x<mask>, FUSE_OK, LOCK_RD_OK.
. Forbid other LC decodes, the opposite secure-boot outcome, FUSE_SECRETS_NOT_LOCKED, BL1 lock failures and any MANIFEST_ERR=/MANIFEST_ALL_FAILED/LC_STATE_INVALID=.
. Assert a single primary attempt in ROM order, with the LC and SBOOT_DIS echoes before it.
. Read LOCKS[31:0] from efuse_shadow_probe_o and assert all six secret read-lock bits are set.

*Checkers:*

[cols="2,5",options="header"]
|===
|Checker |Contract
|`CHK-FUSE-LOCK-STIMULUS` |The staged image has the expected LC, SBOOT_DIS, LOCKS=0, LOCKS_SPARE=0 and clean key fuses.
|`CHK-MANIFEST-PRECONDITION` |Each slot has the enforced bit, a plaintext payload and empty selector_bits.
|`CHK-MANIFEST-LIFECYCLE` |The planted lifecycle constraint landed and is covered by manifest_hash.
|`CHK-ROM-PATH` |Inherited: every required marker observed and no forbidden marker printed.
|`CHK-VERDICT` |Inherited: BL1 completes with PASS through cold_scratch[0].
|`CHK-DECISION-ORDER` |One accepted primary attempt occurs in ROM order with the LC and SBOOT_DIS echoes before it.
|`CHK-FUSE-LOCK-REGISTER` |LOCKS[31:0] from the shadow probe has all six secret read-lock bits set.
|===

*Anti-vacuity:* The staged image must carry LOCKS=0 so set locks can only come from the ROM, the manifest names only the cell's lifecycle so a wrong sensed state refuses the slot, and BL1's FUSE_CHK proves hand-off.

*Observation:* Console markers (ROM and BL1 LOCKS echo), efuse_shadow_probe_o LOCKS word, cold_scratch[0] verdict.

*Members:*

[cols="3,4,4,4,2",options="header"]
|===
|Test |Stimulus |Expected |Checkers it adds |Requirements
|`sep_firmware_fuse_lock_secure_test_dev_test` |sep_efuse_lc_test_dev.toml (LC raw 0x0, SBOOT_DIS=0, LOCKS=0) with the signed image, lifecycle constraint TEST_DEV only. DV shortcut: +esrc_noise_force (ESRC noise input forced; ring oscillators do not run in sim) |Boots BL1 through PUBK_AUTHORIZED, RSA_EXEC, RSA_VERIFY_OK with FUSE_SECRETS_LOCKED and all six LOCKS read-lock bits set; SBOOT_OFF absent. |None beyond the group |`SEP-ROM-HOFF-030`, `SEP-ROM-SB-020`
|`sep_firmware_fuse_lock_secure_prod_test` |sep_efuse_lc_prod.toml (LC raw 0x1, SBOOT_DIS=0, LOCKS=0) with the signed image, lifecycle constraint PROD only. DV shortcut: +esrc_noise_force (ESRC noise input forced; ring oscillators do not run in sim) |Boots BL1 through the RSA chain with LC=PROD, FUSE_SECRETS_LOCKED and all six read-lock bits set; SBOOT_OFF absent. |None beyond the group |`SEP-ROM-HOFF-030`
|`sep_firmware_fuse_lock_non_secure_test_dev_test` |sep_efuse_lc_test_dev_sboot_dis.toml (LC raw 0x0, SBOOT_DIS=1, LOCKS=0) with the unsigned image, lifecycle constraint TEST_DEV only. |Boots BL1 with SBOOT_OFF after PAYLOAD_OK, no PUBK_AUTHORIZED, PUBK_SEL=, PUBK_REVOKE=, RSA_EXEC, RSA_VERIFY_OK, FUSE_VER= or ENTROPY_OK, FUSE_SECRETS_LOCKED and all six read-lock bits set. |None beyond the group |`SEP-ROM-HOFF-030`
|`sep_firmware_fuse_lock_non_secure_prod_test` |sep_efuse_lc_prod_sboot_dis.toml (LC raw 0x1, SBOOT_DIS=1, LOCKS=0) with the unsigned image, lifecycle constraint PROD only. |Boots BL1 with LC=PROD and SBOOT_OFF, no secure-only markers, FUSE_SECRETS_LOCKED and all six read-lock bits set. |None beyond the group |`SEP-ROM-HOFF-030`, `SEP-ROM-SB-040`
|===

==== Manifest validation and slot retry
[[sep-rom-vplan-primary-fail-backup]]
===== Primary refusal with backup boot

*Tests:* 18.

*Base:* `hw/sys/sep/dv/cocotb/tests/rom_fw/sep_primary_fail_backup_boot_base.py`.

*Objective:* A primary SPI slot refused for exactly one planted defect fails over to a fully sealed backup slot that boots BL1 under PROD-enforced secure boot.

*Procedure:*

. Stage the member's +sep_efuse_preload and assert LC raw 0x1 (PROD) and SBOOT_DIS=0, plus member fuse preconditions (BL1_VERSION, CHIPLET_PUBK_REVOKE, CLASS_KEY).
. Start the ESRC noise driver and the ROM virt-console capture.
. Read the signed OCA image (oca_secure_boot.bin unless overridden), apply corrupt_primary() to the slot at 0x1000 and optional prepare_backup() to the slot at 0x41000.
. Assert offline that the backup is still sealed (payload/TOC/hash-chain/manifest_hash/RSA) and its modulus hashes to ROM key slot 0.
. Preload OcahSpiFlash, seed the SPI boot strap (+sep_boot_from_spi) and boot VeeR from the Boot ROM.
. Poll cold_scratch[0] until the BL1 PASS verdict (24M-cycle backstop).
. Assert required markers (BOOT_SPI, MANIFEST_SRC=0x00001000, MANIFEST_OK, PAYLOAD_OK, LC=PROD) and forbidden markers (WAIT_SMC_MANIFEST, SBOOT_OFF, MANIFEST_ALL_FAILED, FUSE: SBOOT_DIS: 1).
. Split the console into slot attempts and check primary error code, stage, ordered/absent markers and defect-marker position, then the backup accepted chain and total RSA_EXEC/RSA_VERIFY_OK counts.
. Check the flash transaction log shows a read covering 0x1000 before one covering 0x41000.

*Checkers:*

[cols="2,5",options="header"]
|===
|Checker |Contract
|`CHK-STIMULUS-EFUSE` |Asserts the staged OTP is PROD with SBOOT_DIS=0 so the secure-boot verdict is reachable.
|`CHK-STIMULUS-BACKUP-SEALED` |Logged after verify_sealed/verify_public_key assert the backup passes every hash and its signature offline.
|`CHK-ROM-PATH` |Inherited: every required console marker is observed and no forbidden marker appears.
|`CHK-VERDICT` |Inherited from sep_base_test.poll_boot: firmware completes with PASS through cold_scratch[0].
|`CHK-PRIMARY-FAILOVER` |Exactly two attempts (0x1000 then 0x41000); primary ends with the declared code at the declared stage with ordered/absent markers; backup accepted; RSA_EXEC and RSA_VERIFY_OK counts equal 1 plus the primary's declared counts.
|`CHK-FAILOVER-ADDR` |Asserts the flash model served a read covering 0x1000 before one covering 0x41000.
|===

*Anti-vacuity:* Non-empty required_markers and console are asserted, the class contract rejects a member without an error code or defect marker, and the defect marker must be the last or second-last line of the primary attempt and appear exactly once.

*Observation:* ROM virt-console markers (MANIFEST_PRIMARY/BACKUP, MANIFEST_SRC=, MANIFEST_ERR=, PUBK_\*, RSA_\*, PAYLOAD_OK), cold_scratch[0] verdict, OcahSpiFlash transaction history, staged eFuse fields.

*Members:*

[cols="3,4,4,4,2",options="header"]

|Test |Stimulus |Expected |Checkers it adds |Requirements
|`sep_firmware_primary_invalid_key_hash_test` |Runs the base procedure of <<sep-rom-vplan-ot-spi-boot>>. Byte 0 of the primary's RSA-3072 modulus inverted (XOR 0xFF) and manifest_hash recomputed (signature left stale); PROD preload sep_efuse_lc_prod.toml with BL1_VERSION=0 and no revocation; backup binds slot 0. DV shortcut: +esrc_noise_force (ring-oscillator noise input forced; ESRC raw noise driven by esrc_noise_task) |primary refused with PUBK_UNAUTHORIZED / OCA_FAIL_ROOT_KEY_UNAUTHORIZED (MANIFEST_ERR=0x00030023) before RSA_EXEC; backup verifies (RSA_EXEC, RSA_VERIFY_OK) and boots. |`CHK-STIMULUS-EFUSE`, `CHK-KEYHASH-FAILOVER`, `CHK-KEYHASH-ADDR` |`SEP-ROM-SB-050`, `SEP-ROM-RETRY-030`

[[sep-rom-vplan-backup-manifest-fail]]
===== Backup manifest refusal, terminal

*Tests:* 16.

*Base:* `hw/sys/sep/dv/cocotb/tests/rom_fw/sep_backup_manifest_fail_base.py`.

*Objective:* Proves that when the primary SPI slot is refused and the backup carries one planted defect, the ROM refuses the backup for that exact reason and halts terminally without booting.

*Procedure:*

. Load a PROD OTP preload (LC raw 0x1, SBOOT_DIS=0) via +sep_efuse_preload; subclass check_efuse() pins BL1_VERSION/CHIPLET_PUBK_REVOKE.
. Load the signed flash image oca_secure_boot.bin into OcahSpiFlash; break the primary magic (default) and plant the subclass defect in the backup slot (0x41000), re-hashing where needed.
. Start esrc_noise_task and the ROM virt-console decoder; copy ROM ITCM/DTCM hex and pulse tcm_load_i.
. Boot the CPU from Boot ROM with +sep_boot_from_spi and poll scratch_cold_probe_o every clock (cold_scratch[1] status, cold_scratch[0] verdict) for up to 24M cycles.
. After the verdict, watch 20k more cycles for cold_scratch[1] movement or new console lines.
. Judge console order: primary MANIFEST_SRC and primary MANIFEST_ERR both before backup MANIFEST_SRC, and the defect marker after the backup read; require the terminal MANIFEST_ERR code.
. Judge cold_scratch[1] held STATUS_ENCODE(ERROR, status_for_result(code)) and verdict is FAIL; forbid SBOOT_OFF, PRE_JUMP, BL1_COPIED, BL1_JUMP= and subclass extras.

*Checkers:*

[cols="2,5",options="header"]
|===
|Checker |Contract
|`CHK-STIMULUS-EFUSE` |LC_STATE raw equals expected_lc_raw (PROD) and SBOOT_DIS is clear before the run.
|`CHK-FAILOVER-PRIMARY` |Primary slot MANIFEST_SRC=0x00001000 is read and refused with primary_expected_error.
|`CHK-FAILOVER-BACKUP` |Backup MANIFEST_SRC=0x00041000 is read after the primary read and after the primary rejection.
|`CHK-BACKUP-DEFECT` |backup_defect_marker appears after the backup read line.
|`CHK-TERMINAL` |MANIFEST_ERR=expected_error printed, cold_scratch[1] held 0x0F01_0000 OR SEP_MSG, ROM signalled completion with FAIL.
|`CHK-NO-BOOT` |None of SBOOT_OFF, PRE_JUMP, BL1_COPIED, BL1_JUMP= or extra_forbidden appear.
|`CHK-HANG` |cold_scratch[1] and the console stay still for 20000 cycles after the terminal verdict.
|===

*Anti-vacuity:* Asserts the core retired instructions, the console is non-empty, the primary rejection precedes the backup read, and the defect marker follows the backup read.

*Observation:* Virt-console markers (MANIFEST_SRC/ERR, PUBK_\*, RSA_\*), cold_scratch[1] status sequence and cold_scratch[0] verdict via scratch_cold_probe_o, cpu_trace_valid_o retire count; expected status word is derived by parsing the ROM's own status_for_result() in oca_boot.c.

[[sep-rom-vplan-structural-fail]]
===== Structural refusal of both slots

*Tests:* 12.

*Base:* `hw/sys/sep/dv/cocotb/tests/rom_fw/sep_backup_manifest_structural_fail_base.py`.

*Objective:* Both manifest slots are refused in the manifest stage before key selection, and the ROM halts with the backup's own error code and a FAIL verdict.

*Procedure:*

. Select the committed PROD preload (sep_efuse_lc_prod.toml); assert LC raw 0x1, SBOOT_DIS=0 and clean key fuses, then write it.
. Load the signed oca_secure_boot.bin; corrupt the primary (default break_magic, OCA_FAIL_MAGIC 0x00030002) and plant the member's backup defect (resealed where the field is signed).
. Backdoor-load the ROM itcm/dtcm hex through tcm_load_i, start ESRC noise and release the CPU at the Boot ROM base with +sep_boot_from_spi.
. Poll cold_scratch[0]/[1] every cycle until a verdict (24M-cycle limit), then watch 20k cycles for quiescence.
. Split the console into slot attempts; require exactly primary 0x1000 then backup 0x41000 with MANIFEST_PRIMARY/MANIFEST_BACKUP headers once each.
. Assert each attempt's error code, ordered and absent markers (no PUBK_SEL/PUBK_AUTHORIZED/PUBK_REVOKE/FUSE_VER/RSA_EXEC) and MANIFEST_ERR= exactly twice.
. Assert MANIFEST_ALL_FAILED once after the backup, cold_scratch[1] holds STATUS_ENCODE(ERROR, status_for_result(code)), fw_done with FAIL, and no SBOOT_OFF/BL1 progress markers.
. Members check that the flash BFM served the planted field bytes.

*Checkers:*

[cols="2,5",options="header"]
|===
|Checker |Contract
|`CHK-STIMULUS-EFUSE` |OTP is PROD with SBOOT_DIS clear.
|`CHK-FAILOVER-PRIMARY` |The primary attempt stops at the manifest stage with primary_expected_error after its ordered markers and without key-selection markers.
|`CHK-BACKUP-DEFECT` |The backup attempt stops at the manifest stage with expected_error, its defect marker is the error line or the line before it, and MANIFEST_ERR= appears exactly twice.
|`CHK-TERMINAL` |MANIFEST_ALL_FAILED follows the backup, cold_scratch[1] holds the mapped ERROR word and the verdict is FAIL.
|`CHK-NO-BOOT` |No SBOOT_OFF, PRE_JUMP, BL1_COPIED, BL1_JUMP= or member-forbidden marker appears.
|`CHK-HANG` |cold_scratch[1] and the console do not move for 20k cycles after the verdict.
|===

*Anti-vacuity:* Asserts retired>0 and a non-empty console, the exact two-attempt list, and a contract check that expected_error differs from primary_expected_error so the terminal code identifies the backup.

*Observation:* ROM console per-attempt markers and MANIFEST_ERR= codes, cold_scratch[1] status sequence, cold_scratch[0] verdict, post-verdict quiescence, and flash BFM served bytes.

[[sep-rom-vplan-primary-length]]
===== Primary manifest length mismatch

*Tests:* 5.

*Base:* `hw/sys/sep/dv/cocotb/tests/rom_fw/sep_primary_manifest_length_fail_base.py`.

*Objective:* A primary whose manifest_length differs from the body size its magic selects is refused with OCA_FAIL_MANIFEST_LENGTH before any RSA work, and the sealed backup boots.

*Procedure:*

. Select the PROD preload (sep_efuse_lc_prod.toml); assert LC raw 0x1, SBOOT_DIS=0, BL1_VERSION=0 and no revocation.
. Load the signed oca_secure_boot.bin into the SPI flash BFM and rewrite the primary manifest_version_minor/manifest_length to the member's value (rehashed).
. Verify the backup is fully sealed and binds ROM key slot 0 (members may rewrite and reseal it).
. Boot from the Boot ROM over OT-SPI (+sep_boot_from_spi, +esrc_noise_force) and poll cold_scratch[0] for PASS.
. Split the console into attempts: primary refused at the manifest stage after OCA_BODY= and MFST_VER= with MANIFEST_ERR=0x0003001c and no PUBK_SEL/PUBK_AUTHORIZED/RSA_EXEC.
. Require the backup accepted through PUBK_AUTHORIZED, RSA_EXEC, RSA_VERIFY_OK, MANIFEST_OK, PAYLOAD_OK, with one RSA_EXEC/RSA_VERIFY_OK in total plus BL1_COPIED and BL1_JUMP=.
. Assert flash reads served the primary before the backup and served the planted version/length bytes.

*Checkers:*

[cols="2,5",options="header"]
|===
|Checker |Contract
|`CHK-PRIMARY-FAILOVER` |Primary refused at the manifest stage with 0x0003001c after OCA_BODY=/MFST_VER=; backup accepted in the full verify order; RSA marker counts as declared.
|`CHK-FAILOVER-ADDR` |The device served the primary read before the backup read.
|`CHK-STIMULUS-SERVED` |The flash BFM served the planted major/minor/manifest_length bytes for the primary.
|`CHK-STIMULUS-BACKUP-SEALED` |Backup passes payload_hash, every TOC image digest, the hash chain, manifest_hash and offline RSA verification, and its modulus hashes to the digest of the ROM slot it selects.
|`CHK-STIMULUS-LENGTH` |The primary starts v1.0 with body-size length and OCAC magic, and reads back the planted (major, minor, length) after the rehash.
|===

*Anti-vacuity:* `+__init_subclass__+` rejects a primary_length equal to the body size, the attempt list must be exactly [0x1000, 0x41000], and the served-field check proves the planted bytes reached the ROM.

*Observation:* ROM console attempt markers and MANIFEST_ERR= codes, RSA_EXEC/RSA_VERIFY_OK counts, SPI read order and served bytes, cold_scratch[0] verdict.

*Members:*

[[sep-rom-vplan-backup-length]]
===== Backup manifest length mismatch

*Tests:* 3.

*Base:* `hw/sys/sep/dv/cocotb/tests/rom_fw/sep_backup_manifest_length_fail_base.py`.

*Objective:* A backup declaring a manifest_length other than the classic body size at minor version 0 or 1 is refused with OCA_FAIL_MANIFEST_LENGTH, and the ROM halts after both slots fail structurally.

*Procedure:*

. Load PROD preload sep_efuse_lc_prod.toml and assert LC raw 0x1, SBOOT_DIS=0, BL1_VERSION=0 and CHIPLET_PUBK_REVOKE=0 (CHK-STIMULUS-EFUSE).
. Break the primary magic; on the backup (still OCAC v1.0) set manifest_version_minor and manifest_length per the member, re-hashed.
. Serve the image over OcahSpiFlash, start esrc noise and console decoder, load ROM TCMs and boot from Boot ROM with +sep_boot_from_spi.
. Poll cold_scratch[1]/[0] until the verdict, then watch 20k cycles for quiescence.
. Judge per-attempt console: MANIFEST_PRIMARY, primary BAD_MAGIC with no OCA_BODY=, MANIFEST_BACKUP, backup OCA_BODY=, MFST_VER=, MANIFEST_LENGTH error, then MANIFEST_ALL_FAILED once.
. Judge no key/RSA markers, exactly two MANIFEST_ERR= lines, cold_scratch[1]=0x0F010009, FAIL verdict, no boot-progress markers.
. Judge the device served the planted major/minor/length bytes for the backup.

*Checkers:*

[cols="2,5",options="header"]
|===
|Checker |Contract
|`CHK-STIMULUS-EFUSE` |OTP is PROD with SBOOT_DIS clear, BL1_VERSION=0 and CHIPLET_PUBK_REVOKE=0.
|`CHK-STIMULUS-LENGTH` |Backup starts v1.0 with body-size length and OCAC magic, and reads back the planted (major, minor, length).
|`CHK-FAILOVER-PRIMARY` |Primary attempt refused with BAD_MAGIC at the manifest stage, with no OCA_BODY= and no key/RSA markers.
|`CHK-BACKUP-DEFECT` |Backup attempt OCA_BODY=, MFST_VER=, MANIFEST_LENGTH error as last line, no key/RSA markers; MANIFEST_ERR= twice, MANIFEST_OK/RSA_EXEC zero.
|`CHK-TERMINAL` |cold_scratch[1] held 0x0F010009, MANIFEST_ALL_FAILED once after the backup error, FAIL verdict.
|`CHK-NO-BOOT` |SBOOT_OFF, PRE_JUMP, BL1_COPIED, BL1_JUMP= absent.
|`CHK-STIMULUS-SERVED` |One flash read returned exactly the planted backup version/length bytes.
|`CHK-HANG` |Status and console quiet for 20000 cycles after the verdict (inherited).
|===

*Anti-vacuity:* The class contract requires a backup_length different from the consumer body size at import and distinct primary/backup error codes at construction; the core must retire instructions and the console must be non-empty.

*Observation:* Per-MANIFEST_SRC console attempts (OCA_BODY=, MFST_VER=, MANIFEST_ERR=), MANIFEST_PRIMARY/BACKUP/ALL_FAILED headers, cold_scratch[1] status sequence and cold_scratch[0] verdict, OcahSpiFlash read bytes.

|`sep_firmware_backup_manifest_major_version_valid_minor_0_length_incorrect_test` |Backup minor=0, manifest_length = body size + 4. DV shortcut: esrc_noise_task drives the ESRC noise port (no +esrc_noise_force plusarg, so OTBN is not expected to run) |Primary OCA_FAIL_MAGIC; backup OCA_FAIL_MANIFEST_LENGTH; cold_scratch[1]=0x0F010009, MANIFEST_ALL_FAILED, FAIL, halt. |None beyond the group |`SEP-ROM-MAN-030`, `SEP-ROM-RETRY-030`, `SEP-ROM-RETRY-050`

[[sep-rom-vplan-device-id]]
===== Device-identity selector bytes

*Tests:* 2.

*Base:* `hw/sys/sep/dv/cocotb/tests/rom_fw/sep_device_id_variation_base.py`.

*Objective:* A manifest identity byte is compared against the fuse only when selector_bits selects it, so a primary with a selected mismatching byte above a selected matching byte is refused and a backup whose only mismatching byte is unselected boots.

*Procedure:*

. Stage sep_efuse_lc_prod.toml, assert PROD, SBOOT_DIS=0 and clean key fuses, and read the fuse identity (SEP_CHIPLET_ID or SEP_SIP_ID).
. In the primary, select only match_byte (equal to the fuse) and mismatch_byte (fuse XOR 0xFF); other bytes keep the 0xA5 fill.
. In the backup, select bytes 1..31 equal to the fuse and leave byte 0 unselected (0xA5, different from the fuse).
. Assert the backup is still sealed, then boot from SPI and poll cold_scratch[0] for the BL1 PASS verdict.
. Assert the primary is refused at the manifest stage with OCA_FAIL_<kind>\_ID after OCA_BODY= and MFST_VER=, with no PUBK_SEL=, PUBK_AUTHORIZED, PUBK_REVOKE= or FUSE_VER= in the primary attempt.
. Assert the backup is accepted through the inherited failover checks.
. Assert the flash served the planted selector_bits and identity bytes for both slots.

*Checkers:*

[cols="2,5",options="header"]
|===
|Checker |Contract
|`CHK-STIMULUS-IDENTITY` |Logged after asserting the primary selector and identity bytes hold the planted relation to the fuse.
|`CHK-STIMULUS-BACKUP-IDENTITY` |Logged after asserting the backup selector_bits equals bytes 1..31 and byte 0 fill differs from the fuse.
|`CHK-STIMULUS-SERVED` |One SPI read returned exactly the planted bytes for each recorded selector_bits and identity field.
|`CHK-PRIMARY-FAILOVER` |Primary attempt shows OCA_BODY= then MFST_VER= then the OCA_FAIL_<kind>\_ID MANIFEST_ERR= line with no PUBK_SEL=, PUBK_AUTHORIZED, PUBK_REVOKE= or FUSE_VER=; backup attempt accepted through PAYLOAD_OK; RSA_EXEC and RSA_VERIFY_OK each once.
|`CHK-FAILOVER-ADDR` |The device served 0x1000 before 0x41000.
|`CHK-PER-BYTE-VARIATION` |PASS logged after CHK-PRIMARY-FAILOVER, CHK-FAILOVER-ADDR and CHK-STIMULUS-SERVED pass.
|===

*Anti-vacuity:* The mismatch byte sits above a selected matching byte and the backup's unselected byte 0 differs from the fuse (asserted), and the served-field check proves the planted bytes reached the DUT.

*Observation:* Console attempt markers and MANIFEST_ERR=, cold_scratch[0] verdict, flash transaction bytes for selector_bits and identity fields.

DV shortcut: +esrc_noise_force (ESRC noise input forced; ring oscillators do not run in sim); the TB re-hashes/re-signs the mutated slot with the dev0 key |Primary refused at manifest stage with OCA_FAIL_CHIPLET_ID (0x00030007) before key selection; backup boots BL1. |None beyond the group |`SEP-ROM-MAN-030`, `SEP-ROM-RETRY-030`
|`sep_package_id_per_byte_variations_test` |Primary selects package_id byte 3 (equal to SEP_SIP_ID) and byte 6 (fuse XOR 0xFF); backup selects bytes 1..31 equal to the fuse with byte 0 unselected (0xA5). DV shortcut: +esrc_noise_force (ESRC noise input forced; ring oscillators do not run in sim); the TB re-hashes/re-signs the mutated slot with the dev0 key |Primary refused at manifest stage with OCA_FAIL_PACKAGE_ID (0x00030008) before key selection; backup boots BL1. |None beyond the group |`SEP-ROM-MAN-030`, `SEP-ROM-RETRY-030`

==== Key authorization, revocation and RSA

[[sep-rom-vplan-rom-key-revoked-backup]]
===== Revoked ROM key on the backup

*Tests:* 6.

*Base:* `hw/sys/sep/dv/cocotb/tests/rom_fw/sep_pubkey_rom_revoked_base.py`.

*Objective:* Proves a backup manifest that selects a device-revoked ROM key slot is refused before RSA, and with the primary also refused the ROM halts.

*Procedure:*

. Load PROD preload sep_efuse_lc_prod_pubk_revoke<N>.toml; assert CHIPLET_PUBK_REVOKE == 1\<<N, BL1_VERSION == 0, SBOOT_DIS clear.
. Break the primary manifest magic (mm.break_magic).
. Anchor the backup on ROM key slot N: shipped slot for N=0, else graft the backup slot of oca_rom_key<N>\_boot.bin; verify selector, key digest and full seal.
. Preload the SPI flash BFM with the signed image and start esrc_noise_task and rom_console_task.
. Backdoor-load TCM and release the core at the ROM base; poll cold_scratch[0]/[1] for up to 24M cycles.
. Watch 20k cycles after the verdict for status/console quiescence.
. Assert primary rejection, failover ordering, KEY_REVOKED code, status word, FAIL verdict and forbidden markers (incl. RSA_EXEC, RSA_VERIFY_OK).

*Checkers:*

[cols="2,5",options="header"]
|===
|Checker |Contract
|`CHK-STIMULUS-EFUSE` |Logged after asserting LC raw, SBOOT_DIS clear and the per-slot revoke bitmap.
|`CHK-FAILOVER-PRIMARY` |primary read at 0x1000 and rejected with MANIFEST_ERR for OCA_FAIL_MAGIC.
|`CHK-FAILOVER-BACKUP` |backup read after the primary rejection.
|`CHK-SECURE-RAN` |SBOOT_OFF absent.
|`CHK-BACKUP-DEFECT` |KEY_REVOKED MANIFEST_ERR appears after the backup read.
|`CHK-TERMINAL` |MANIFEST_ERR=KEY_REVOKED, cold_scratch[1] = ERROR OR status_for_result(code), fw_done with fw_pass=0.
|`CHK-NO-BOOT` |none of PRE_JUMP/BL1_COPIED/BL1_JUMP= or the extra forbidden key/RSA markers.
|`CHK-HANG` |cold_scratch[1] and console unchanged for 20k cycles after the verdict.
|`CHK-REVOKE-ECHO` |console shows PUBK_SEL=N and PUBK_REVOKE=1\<<N, KEY_REVOKED exactly once.
|===

*Anti-vacuity:* retired>0 and non-empty console asserts, plus a backup that is fully sealed and authorized so revocation is the only possible refusal.

*Observation:* ROM console markers (MANIFEST_SRC=, MANIFEST_ERR=, PUBK_SEL=, PUBK_REVOKE=), cold_scratch[1] status sequence, cold_scratch[0] verdict.

[[sep-rom-vplan-rom-key-revoked-primary]]
===== ROM key revocation and selection on the primary

*Tests:* 9.

*Base:* `hw/sys/sep/dv/cocotb/tests/rom_fw/sep_pubkey_rom_revoked_primary_base.py`.

*Objective:* Proves a fuse-revoked ROM key slot (CHIPLET_PUBK_REVOKE bit n) refuses a fully valid manifest that selects it before RSA, with a failover outcome when only the primary uses it and a terminal one when both do, and that a primary selecting an unrevoked ROM key slot verifies and boots without failover.

*Procedure:*

. Load PROD preload sep_efuse_lc_prod_pubk_revoke<n>.toml with exactly bit n of CHIPLET_PUBK_REVOKE set and BL1_VERSION=0.
. For n!=0 graft the primary slot from oca_rom_key<n>\_boot.bin so it selects and is signed by ROM key n; for n=0 keep the shipped primary.
. Assert both slots verify_sealed/verify_public_key and that the backup's selector (slot 0) is or is not revoked as the member's outcome requires.
. Serve the image over OcahSpiFlash with +sep_boot_from_spi and boot the production Boot ROM.
. Failover members (n=1..5): poll to BL1 PASS on cold_scratch[0]; judge primary attempt PUBK_SEL=n, PUBK_AUTHORIZED, PUBK_REVOKE=, KEY_REVOKED and backup attempt accepted.
. Terminal member (n=0): poll scratch_cold_probe_o; judge both slots refused with OCA_FAIL_ROOT_KEY_REVOKED, MANIFEST_ALL_FAILED and FAIL verdict.
. Check flash transactions: primary manifest read precedes the backup read.

*Checkers:*

[cols="2,5",options="header"]
|===
|Checker |Contract
|`CHK-STIMULUS-REVOKED-SLOT` |Primary selector equals the revoked slot, grafted only for n!=0, and the slot is sealed.
|`CHK-STIMULUS-OUTCOME-SHAPE` |Backup selector's revoked state matches the member's TERMINAL/FAILOVER base.
|`CHK-REVOKE-FAILOVER` |Failover: primary attempt ends PUBK_REVOKE= then KEY_REVOKED; backup attempt accepted through PAYLOAD_OK; revoke echo twice, selector and error once.
|`CHK-STIMULUS-BOTH-SEALED` |Terminal: backup selects the revoked slot and passes verify_sealed/verify_public_key.
|`CHK-BOTH-REVOKED` |Terminal: KEY_REVOKED error appears exactly twice, straddling the backup read.
|`CHK-BOTH-SLOTS-REFUSED` |Terminal: PUBK_SEL and PUBK_REVOKE echoes twice, error twice straddling the backup read, MANIFEST_ALL_FAILED.
|`CHK-BOTH-FETCHED` |Terminal: device served the primary then the backup manifest address.
|`CHK-PRIMARY-FAILOVER` |Failover (inherited): per-attempt order, RSA_EXEC/RSA_VERIFY_OK counts 1, defect marker once.
|`CHK-FAILOVER-ADDR` |Failover (inherited): device served 0x1000 before 0x41000.
|`CHK-FAILOVER-PRIMARY` |Terminal (inherited): primary read and refused with KEY_REVOKED before the backup read.
|`CHK-TERMINAL` |Terminal (inherited): cold_scratch[1] held 0x0F01000C, fw_done with fw_pass=0.
|`CHK-NO-BOOT` |Terminal (inherited): no PRE_JUMP, BL1_COPIED, BL1_JUMP=, RSA_EXEC, RSA_VERIFY_OK or PUBK_\* failure marker.
|`CHK-HANG` |Terminal (inherited): cold_scratch[1] and the console stay unchanged for 20k cycles after the verdict.
|===

*Anti-vacuity:* Pre-run asserts that the primary is sealed and authorized and that CHIPLET_PUBK_REVOKE is exactly bit n; failover members require PUBK_AUTHORIZED before PUBK_REVOKE= and every member forbids PUBK_UNAUTHORIZED, so the refusal is the fuse, not the key.

*Observation:* Virt-console markers per MANIFEST_SRC attempt (PUBK_SEL=, PUBK_AUTHORIZED, PUBK_REVOKE=, MANIFEST_ERR=, RSA_\*), cold_scratch[0] verdict and cold_scratch[1] status, OcahSpiFlash read transactions.

[[sep-rom-vplan-chiplet-key]]
===== Chiplet fused keys

*Tests:* 4.

*Base:* `hw/sys/sep/dv/cocotb/tests/rom_fw/sep_chiplet_pubkey_base.py`.

*Objective:* Proves that a manifest selecting CHIPLET fused key 0 or 1 is authenticated against that fuse digest and boots from the primary when the key is unrevoked, and that the matching CHIPLET_PUBK_REVOKE bit refuses both slots before RSA.

*Procedure:*

. Load the per-member PROD preload sep_efuse_lc_prod_chiplet_key\{0,1}[\_revoke].toml and assert LC 0x1, SBOOT_DIS=0, BL1_VERSION=0.
. Assert CHIPLET_PUBK_REVOKE is exactly bit 0 (ROM dev key 0, counterfactual) plus bit 16/17 for revoke members.
. Assert the selected CHIPLET_PUBK_HASHn equals the little-endian dev0 digest and the other hash fuse holds a non-zero decoy.
. In both slots, verify the golden seal and signer, set public_key_sel to PUBK_SEL_FUSE_KEY_n (slot 16/17), re-seal with dev0, and re-verify sealed.
. Boot from SPI (valid: ot_dma flow with +esrc_noise_force; revoke: backup_manifest_fail flow with TCM staging, no RSA expected).
. Valid: require PUBK_SEL echo, PUBK_REVOKE=0x00000001, RSA_EXEC, RSA_VERIFY_OK and MANIFEST_OK in order, the echoes and RSA markers exactly once each, BL1 jump and PASS verdict.
. Revoke: require MANIFEST_ERR=OCA_FAIL_ROOT_KEY_REVOKED once per slot straddling the backup read, MANIFEST_ALL_FAILED, terminal FAIL and a halt.
. Check SPI evidence: valid reads nothing in the backup span; revoke fetches primary then backup.

*Checkers:*

[cols="2,5",options="header"]
|===
|Checker |Contract
|`CHK-STIMULUS-FUSED-KEY` |Both slots select the same fused key slot and are re-signed and re-verified sealed.
|`CHK-STIMULUS-CHIPLET-EFUSE` |Revoke bitmap, selected digest and decoy digest match the member's contract.
|`CHK-FUSEKEY-RAN` |Valid: primary MANIFEST_SRC -> PUBK_SEL -> PUBK_REVOKE -> RSA_EXEC -> RSA_VERIFY_OK -> MANIFEST_OK in order, each echo/RSA marker exactly once.
|`CHK-NO-FAILOVER` |Valid: the primary read returns OCAC magic and no SPI read touches the backup slot span.
|`CHK-ROM-PATH` |Valid: required markers present (incl. LC=PROD, BL1_COPIED, BL1_JUMP=) and forbidden PUBK_\*/RSA failure, MANIFEST_ERR=, backup source and SBOOT_OFF absent.
|`CHK-STIMULUS-BOTH-SEALED` |Revoke: the backup selects the revoked slot and passes verify_sealed and verify_public_key.
|`CHK-FAILOVER-PRIMARY` |Revoke: primary read and refused with KEY_REVOKED before the backup read.
|`CHK-FAILOVER-BACKUP` |Revoke: backup read after the primary rejection.
|`CHK-SECURE-RAN` |Revoke: SBOOT_OFF absent.
|`CHK-BOTH-REVOKED` |Revoke: the KEY_REVOKED error line appears exactly twice, straddling the backup read.
|`CHK-TERMINAL` |Revoke: status 0x0F01_0000 OR status_for_result(KEY_REVOKED) seen, fw_done and not fw_pass.
|`CHK-NO-BOOT` |Revoke: no PRE_JUMP/BL1_COPIED/BL1_JUMP= and none of the extra forbidden markers (RSA_EXEC, RSA_VERIFY_OK, PUBK_\* failures, PUBK_SEL=0x00000000).
|`CHK-BOTH-SLOTS-REFUSED` |Revoke: PUBK_SEL and PUBK_REVOKE echoes appear exactly twice and MANIFEST_ALL_FAILED is printed.
|`CHK-BOTH-FETCHED` |Revoke: the SPI device served primary then backup manifest addresses.
|`CHK-HANG` |Revoke: status and console stay still for 20k cycles after the verdict.
|===

*Anti-vacuity:* ROM key 0 is revoked and the other chiplet hash is a non-zero decoy, so a ROM that ignored the selector or read the wrong fuse would fail; valid members forbid any backup-span read, revoke members require two refusals straddling the backup read.

*Observation:* Console PUBK_SEL=/PUBK_REVOKE=/RSA_\*/MANIFEST_ERR= markers and order, cold_scratch[0]/[1], and OcahSpiFlash read spans.

*Members:*

[cols="3,4,4,4,2",options="header"]
|===
|Test |Stimulus |Expected |Checkers it adds |Requirements
|`sep_firmware_chiplet_pubkey_0_test` |Both slots public_key_sel=slot 16 (PUBK_SEL_FUSE_KEY_0), re-signed with dev0; CHIPLET_PUBK_HASH0=dev0 digest, HASH1=decoy, CHIPLET_PUBK_REVOKE=0x1. DV shortcut: +esrc_noise_force (cocotb drives the entropy decorrelator inputs) |Primary: PUBK_SEL=0x00000010, PUBK_REVOKE=0x00000001, RSA_VERIFY_OK, MANIFEST_OK; boots BL1 from primary with no backup read. |None beyond the group |`SEP-ROM-SB-050`, `SEP-ROM-SB-060`
|`sep_firmware_chiplet_pubkey_1_test` |Both slots public_key_sel=slot 17 (PUBK_SEL_FUSE_KEY_1), re-signed with dev0; CHIPLET_PUBK_HASH1=dev0 digest, HASH0=decoy, CHIPLET_PUBK_REVOKE=0x1. DV shortcut: +esrc_noise_force (cocotb drives the entropy decorrelator inputs) |Primary: PUBK_SEL=0x00000011, PUBK_REVOKE=0x00000001, RSA_VERIFY_OK, MANIFEST_OK; boots BL1 from primary with no backup read. |None beyond the group |`SEP-ROM-SB-050`, `SEP-ROM-SB-060`
|`sep_firmware_chiplet_pubkey_0_revoke_test` |Same re-signed slot-16 image as the key 0 positive test; CHIPLET_PUBK_REVOKE=0x00010001. DV shortcut: tcm_load_i backdoor stages ROM .itcm/.dtcm hex with valid ECC before reset |Both slots refused OCA_BOOT_ERR_BASE OR OCA_FAIL_ROOT_KEY_REVOKED with no RSA_EXEC; MANIFEST_ALL_FAILED; terminal FAIL and halt. |None beyond the group |`SEP-ROM-SB-060`, `SEP-ROM-RETRY-050`
|`sep_firmware_chiplet_pubkey_1_revoke_test` |Same re-signed slot-17 image as the key 1 positive test; CHIPLET_PUBK_REVOKE=0x00020001. DV shortcut: tcm_load_i backdoor stages ROM .itcm/.dtcm hex with valid ECC before reset |Both slots refused OCA_BOOT_ERR_BASE OR OCA_FAIL_ROOT_KEY_REVOKED with no RSA_EXEC; MANIFEST_ALL_FAILED; terminal FAIL and halt. |None beyond the group |`SEP-ROM-SB-060`, `SEP-ROM-RETRY-050`

[[sep-rom-vplan-backup-payload-fail]]
===== Backup payload refusal, terminal

*Tests:* 7.

*Base:* `hw/sys/sep/dv/cocotb/tests/rom_fw/sep_backup_payload_fail_base.py`.

*Objective:* Proves a backup slot that verifies (RSA_VERIFY_OK, MANIFEST_OK) but carries a payload/placement defect is refused with its own code after a primary refusal, and the ROM halts.

*Procedure:*

. Load the PROD preload named by the member (sep_efuse_lc_prod or \_class_key); assert LC=PROD and SBOOT_DIS clear.
. Corrupt the primary (default break_magic; members override) and plant the backup payload defect; slots re-sealed as needed.
. Preload the signed flash image into OcahSpiFlash and start esrc_noise_task (+esrc_noise_force) and rom_console_task.
. Backdoor-load TCM and release the core; poll cold_scratch[0]/[1] up to 24M cycles.
. Watch 20k cycles after the verdict for quiescence.
. Split the console per MANIFEST_SRC= attempt and grade each attempt's ordered/absent markers and error code.
. Assert marker counts, MANIFEST_ALL_FAILED once after the backup, status word, FAIL verdict and no boot progress.

*Checkers:*

[cols="2,5",options="header"]
|===
|Checker |Contract
|`CHK-STIMULUS-EFUSE` |LC raw and SBOOT_DIS asserts hold.
|`CHK-FAILOVER-PRIMARY` |Primary attempt ends with primary_expected_error at its declared stage with ordered/absent markers.
|`CHK-BACKUP-PAYLOAD` |Backup attempt shows PUBK_AUTHORIZED->RSA_EXEC->RSA_VERIFY_OK->MANIFEST_OK->defect marker, ends with expected_error, exact marker counts.
|`CHK-TERMINAL` |cold_scratch[1] held ERROR\|status_for_result(expected_error), fw_done with fw_pass=0.
|`CHK-NO-BOOT` |SBOOT_OFF and BL1 progress markers absent.
|`CHK-HANG` |status and console quiet 20k cycles after the verdict.
|===

*Anti-vacuity:* retired>0, non-empty console, exactly two MANIFEST_SRC= attempts (primary then backup) and MANIFEST_ALL_FAILED once after the backup.

*Observation:* Per-attempt ROM console markers, MANIFEST_ERR= codes, cold_scratch[1] status sequence, cold_scratch[0] verdict, SPI BFM served bytes (members).

*Members:*

[cols="3,4,4,4,2",options="header"]

[[sep-rom-vplan-no-payload-images]]
===== Empty payload TOC

*Tests:* 1.

*Base:* `hw/sys/sep/dv/cocotb/tests/rom_fw/sep_no_payload_images_base.py`.

*Objective:* A plaintext slot whose payload TOC declares image_count = 0 is refused with OCA_FAIL_PAYLOAD_TOC after its signature verifies.

*Procedure:*

. Select the PROD preload (sep_efuse_lc_prod.toml) with clean key fuses; load the signed plaintext oca_secure_boot.bin and assert both slots are unencrypted.
. Set the primary TOC image_count to 0 with payload_hashed_length at the header-only span, and recompute payload_hash and the signature.
. Assert count_zero is the only broken spec rule and only the image_count field changed in the cleartext payload.
. Boot over OT-SPI (+sep_boot_from_spi, +esrc_noise_force) and poll cold_scratch[0] for PASS.
. Require the primary to verify (PUBK_AUTHORIZED, RSA_EXEC, RSA_VERIFY_OK, MANIFEST_OK) and then be refused at the payload stage with MANIFEST_ERR=0x00030015, with no payload-stage or DECRYPT_OK marker.
. Require the backup accepted, RSA_EXEC/RSA_VERIFY_OK twice each, BL1_COPIED and BL1_JUMP=, primary read before backup, and the device served image_count = 0.

*Checkers:*

[cols="2,5",options="header"]
|===
|Checker |Contract
|`CHK-STIMULUS-NO-PAYLOAD-IMAGES` |image_count changed from non-zero to 0, count_zero is the only rule broken and only that field changed.
|`CHK-PRIMARY-FAILOVER` |Primary verified and refused at the payload stage with 0x00030015; backup accepted in full verify order.
|`CHK-FAILOVER-ADDR` |The device served the primary read before the backup read.
|`CHK-STIMULUS-SERVED` |The flash BFM served the zero image_count at the primary TOC offset.
|`CHK-STIMULUS-BACKUP-SEALED` |Backup passes every seal and binds ROM slot 0 before load.
|===

*Anti-vacuity:* spec_rule_violations must equal ['count_zero'], the cleartext diff must sit inside the field, and the served-field check proves the device returned the planted count.

*Observation:* ROM console attempt markers and MANIFEST_ERR= code, RSA marker counts, SPI read order and served TOC bytes, cold_scratch[0] verdict.

*Members:*

[cols="3,4,4,4,2",options="header"]
|===
|Test |Stimulus |Expected |Checkers it adds |Requirements
|`sep_firmware_primary_no_payload_images_test` |Primary TOC image_count = 0 (payload_hashed_length = header-only span, payload_hash and signature recomputed); PROD preload. DV shortcut: +esrc_noise_force (ring-oscillator noise input forced; ESRC raw noise driven by esrc_noise_task) |primary verified, then refused OCA_FAIL_PAYLOAD_TOC (MANIFEST_ERR=0x00030015); backup boots to BL1. |None beyond the group |`SEP-ROM-PL-010`, `SEP-ROM-RETRY-030`
|===

[[sep-rom-vplan-primary-toc]]
===== Primary TOC structural refusal

*Tests:* 12.

*Base:* `hw/sys/sep/dv/cocotb/tests/rom_fw/sep_primary_toc_fail_base.py`.

*Objective:* Proves that a primary slot whose signed TOC carries one planted field defect is refused with the planted code after RSA_VERIFY_OK/MANIFEST_OK (and DECRYPT_OK when encrypted), and the untouched backup then boots BL1.

*Procedure:*

. Load the PROD eFuse preload (plain or \_class_key) and assert LC raw 0x1, SBOOT_DIS=0, BL1_VERSION=0, CHIPLET_PUBK_REVOKE=0.
. Pick the golden image for the TOC defect and assert both slots match the declared encryption flag.
. Plant one TOC field defect in the primary via sep_toc_defect.plant and re-seal it, asserting the exact broken-rule set.
. Assert the backup is fully sealed (payload hash, TOC digests, manifest hash, RSA) and its public key hashes to the ROM slot digest.
. Preload the OcahSpiFlash BFM, start esrc noise and console tasks, and boot the core from ROM with +sep_boot_from_spi (no TCM staging).
. Run poll_boot until the cold_scratch[0] verdict; the scoreboard requires fw_done with PASS.
. Check required markers (BOOT_SPI, MANIFEST_SRC=0x00001000, MANIFEST_OK, PAYLOAD_OK, LC=PROD, BL1_COPIED, BL1_JUMP=) and forbidden markers (WAIT_SMC_MANIFEST, SBOOT_OFF, MANIFEST_ALL_FAILED, FUSE: SBOOT_DIS: 1).
. Grade per-attempt markers: primary refused at payload stage with the planted error, backup accepted with DECRYPT_OK (if encrypted) and PAYLOAD_OK.
. Check SPI transactions: primary read before backup, and the planted primary TOC bytes were served exactly.

*Checkers:*

[cols="2,5",options="header"]
|===
|Checker |Contract
|`CHK-STIMULUS-EFUSE` |PROD LC, SBOOT_DIS=0 and clean key fuses are asserted before the eFuse image is used.
|`CHK-STIMULUS-TOC` |The planted primary TOC breaks exactly the declared spec rule set and changes only allowed cleartext bytes.
|`CHK-STIMULUS-BACKUP-SEALED` |The backup passes verify_sealed and verify_public_key before load.
|`CHK-ROM-PATH` |Each required SPI-path and boot marker appears; forbidden markers do not.
|`CHK-PRIMARY-FAILOVER` |The primary attempt shows PUBK_AUTHORIZED->RSA_EXEC->RSA_VERIFY_OK->MANIFEST_OK(->DECRYPT_OK) then the planted error as its last line; the backup is accepted with PAYLOAD_OK; RSA_EXEC and RSA_VERIFY_OK appear exactly twice.
|`CHK-FAILOVER-ADDR` |The SPI device served the primary manifest address before the backup manifest address.
|`CHK-STIMULUS-SERVED` |One SPI read covered the planted primary TOC field and returned exactly the planted bytes.
|`CHK-VERDICT` |poll_boot logs the cold_scratch[0] verdict; the scoreboard check_phase fails the run unless fw_done and fw_pass.
|===

*Anti-vacuity:* Asserts a non-empty console and non-empty required_markers, exactly two MANIFEST_SRC attempts in order, a payload-stage primary error, and the served planted bytes.

*Observation:* Virtual console markers per attempt, cold_scratch[0] BL1 verdict, and OcahSpiFlash read order and bytes.

[cols="3,4,4,4,2",options="header"]

|Test |Stimulus |Expected |Checkers it adds |Requirements

[[sep-rom-vplan-backup-toc]]
===== Backup TOC structural refusal, terminal

*Tests:* 15.

*Base:* `hw/sys/sep/dv/cocotb/tests/rom_fw/sep_backup_toc_fail_base.py`.

*Objective:* Proves that a backup slot whose signed TOC breaks one structural rule is refused after RSA/MANIFEST_OK (and DECRYPT_OK when encrypted) with the planted code, so with a bad-magic primary the ROM ends terminal in MANIFEST_ALL_FAILED.

*Procedure:*

. Load the PROD eFuse preload (sep_efuse_lc_prod.toml, or sep_efuse_lc_prod_class_key.toml for encrypted members) and assert LC raw 0x1, SBOOT_DIS=0, BL1_VERSION=0, CHIPLET_PUBK_REVOKE=0.
. Pick the golden flash image for the TOC defect (single, multi-image or toc_cap; plaintext or encrypted) and assert both slots match the declared encryption flag.
. Break the primary manifest magic word (OCA_FAIL_MAGIC trigger).
. Plant one TOC field defect in the backup via sep_toc_defect.plant, re-seal the slot, and assert the TOC breaks exactly the expected spec rules and only the allowed cleartext bytes changed.
. Preload the OcahSpiFlash BFM, start esrc noise and console tasks, stage TCM via tcm_load_i, and boot the core from ROM with +sep_boot_from_spi.
. Poll cold_scratch[1] and the verdict in cold_scratch[0] for up to 24M cycles, then watch 20k cycles for quiescence.
. Split the console into MANIFEST_SRC attempts and grade primary (MAGIC, manifest stage) and backup (planted code, payload stage) markers, counts and absences.
. Check the status word STATUS_ENCODE(ERROR, status_for_result(code)), fw_done with FAIL, no boot-progress markers, and that the SPI device served the planted backup TOC bytes.

*Checkers:*

[cols="2,5",options="header"]
|===
|Checker |Contract
|`CHK-STIMULUS-EFUSE` |PROD LC, SBOOT_DIS=0 and clean key fuses are asserted before the eFuse image is written.
|`CHK-STIMULUS-TOC` |The planted backup TOC breaks exactly the declared spec rule set and changes only allowed cleartext bytes.
|`CHK-FAILOVER-PRIMARY` |The primary attempt ends with OCA_FAIL_MAGIC at manifest stage with no RSA_EXEC/RSA_VERIFY_OK/OCA_BODY=.
|`CHK-BACKUP-PAYLOAD` |The backup attempt shows PUBK_AUTHORIZED->RSA_EXEC->RSA_VERIFY_OK->MANIFEST_OK(->DECRYPT_OK) then the planted error as its last line, with exact marker counts and no PAYLOAD_OK or placement markers.
|`CHK-TERMINAL` |cold_scratch[1] held 0x0F01_0000\|status_for_result(code), the ROM signalled done and FAIL, and MANIFEST_ALL_FAILED follows the backup error once.
|`CHK-NO-BOOT` |SBOOT_OFF, PRE_JUMP, BL1_COPIED and BL1_JUMP= never appear.
|`CHK-HANG` |After the verdict, cold_scratch[1] stays constant and the console stays silent for 20k cycles.
|`CHK-STIMULUS-SERVED` |One SPI read covered the planted backup TOC field and returned exactly the planted (cipher/plain) bytes.
|===

*Anti-vacuity:* Asserts instructions retired and a non-empty console, exactly two MANIFEST_SRC attempts (primary then backup), and that the SPI device served the planted bytes.

*Observation:* Virtual console markers per MANIFEST_SRC attempt, cold_scratch[1] status sequence, cold_scratch[0] verdict, post-verdict quiescence, and OcahSpiFlash read transactions.

[[sep-rom-vplan-toc-order]]
===== Non-canonical TOC order

*Tests:* 3.

*Base:* `hw/sys/sep/dv/cocotb/tests/rom_fw/sep_toc_order_boot_base.py`.

*Objective:* Proves a legal but non-canonical TOC arrangement in the primary (BL1 not first, or descending offsets) is accepted on the signed path and BL1 is copied from its own entry's offset.

*Procedure:*

. Select the three-image golden (oca_multi_image_boot.bin or the encrypted variant) and matching PROD / PROD+CLASS_KEY preload; assert LC=PROD, SBOOT_DIS clear, clean key fuses.
. Let the member arrange the primary TOC; verify seal, key and that no spec rule is broken and only TOC bytes changed.
. Compute the expected COPY_SRC from the BL1 entry offset.
. Serve the image from OcahSpiFlash with esrc_noise_task and an OTBN EDN handshake monitor.
. Release the core and poll for the BL1 PASS verdict.
. Assert one primary attempt with PUBK_AUTHORIZED->RSA_EXEC->RSA_VERIFY_OK->MANIFEST_OK->[DECRYPT_OK]->PAYLOAD_OK->COPY_SRC->BL1_COPIED->BL1_JUMP=.

*Checkers:*

[cols="2,5",options="header"]
|===
|Checker |Contract
|`CHK-VERDICT` |BL1 PASS verdict in cold_scratch[0] with PC advance.
|`CHK-ROM-PATH` |Each required marker appears, incl. PUBK_AUTHORIZED, ENTROPY_OK, RSA_EXEC, RSA_VERIFY_OK; SBOOT_OFF forbidden.
|`CHK-OTBN-EDN` |OTBN requested URND and every EDN request was acknowledged without drops.
|`CHK-BL1-BY-TYPE` |The single primary attempt copied BL1 from COPY_SRC = SEP SRAM + payload offset + BL1 entry offset, COPY_SRC printed once.
|===

*Anti-vacuity:* Offline asserts that the arrangement actually differs from canonical (BL1 index > 0 or stored offsets descending) and that the slot breaks no TOC rule.

*Observation:* ROM console markers incl. COPY_SRC=, crypto_edn_req/ack_o, cold_scratch[0] verdict.

*Members:*

[cols="3,4,4,4,2",options="header"]
|===
|Test |Stimulus |Expected |Checkers it adds |Requirements
|`sep_firmware_manifest_primary_reordered_sepbl1_test` |Golden oca_multi_image_boot.bin primary left as packed (SEP_BL1 at index 2); plaintext PROD preload. DV shortcut: +esrc_noise_force (forced ESRC noise input driven by esrc_noise_task). |boots from the primary; COPY_SRC equals SRAM base + payload offset + BL1 entry offset. |`CHK-STIMULUS-TOC-ORDER` |`SEP-ROM-MAN-050`
|`sep_firmware_primary_encrypted_payload_images_descending_order_test` |Encrypted three-image golden, primary TOC entries permuted to golden indices (2,1,0) and re-sealed; PROD+CLASS_KEY preload. DV shortcut: +esrc_noise_force (forced ESRC noise input driven by esrc_noise_task). |boots from the primary with DECRYPT_OK before PAYLOAD_OK; COPY_SRC from BL1's own entry. |`CHK-STIMULUS-TOC-ORDER` |`SEP-ROM-PL-010`, `SEP-ROM-PL-030`
|`sep_firmware_primary_non_encrypted_payload_images_descending_order_test` |Plaintext three-image golden, primary TOC entries permuted to golden indices (2,1,0) and re-sealed; plaintext PROD preload. DV shortcut: +esrc_noise_force (forced ESRC noise input driven by esrc_noise_task). |boots from the primary; COPY_SRC from BL1's own entry. |`CHK-STIMULUS-TOC-ORDER` |`SEP-ROM-PL-010`, `SEP-ROM-PL-040`
|===

[[sep-rom-vplan-decrypt-input]]
===== Decryption input defects

*Tests:* 3.

*Base:* `hw/sys/sep/dv/cocotb/tests/rom_fw/sep_decrypt_input_defect.py`.

*Objective:* A primary with one defective AES-256 decryption input (CLASS_KEY, IV or KDF context) is refused in its payload stage after its signature verifies, and the backup boots.

*Procedure:*

. Pick the row's image (invalid_class_key.bin or oca_encrypted_boot.bin) and PROD preload (sep_efuse_lc_prod_bad_class_key.toml or \_class_key.toml); assert clean key fuses and CLASS_KEY equal to the row's value.
. Assert the shipped primary is encrypted with the golden IV and KDF context and the backup's encryption flag matches the row.
. Plant the row defect in the primary (IV byte 0 or KDF byte 0 XOR 0xFF, resealed; nothing for CLASS_KEY) and predict the outcome with the DV AES/KDF model.
. Boot over OT-SPI (+sep_boot_from_spi, +esrc_noise_force) and poll cold_scratch[0] for PASS.
. Require the primary to verify (PUBK_AUTHORIZED, RSA_EXEC, RSA_VERIFY_OK, MANIFEST_OK) and then print the row's outcome marker and MANIFEST_ERR= code at the payload stage, with no other decrypt outcome.
. Require the backup accepted (with DECRYPT_OK when encrypted), RSA_EXEC/RSA_VERIFY_OK twice each, BL1_COPIED and BL1_JUMP=.
. Count DECRYPT_OK and check the device served the primary's IV and KDF bytes.

*Checkers:*

[cols="2,5",options="header"]
|===
|Checker |Contract
|`CHK-STIMULUS-CLASS-KEY` |OTP CLASS_KEY equals the row's value and differs from (CLASS_KEY row) or equals (IV/KDF rows) the packing key.
|`CHK-STIMULUS-FIELD` |The DV AES/KDF model confirms the planted defect: a wrong IV keeps the pad valid and changes only plaintext byte 0; a wrong KDF input derives a different key and breaks the pad.
|`CHK-PRIMARY-FAILOVER` |Primary verified then refused at the payload stage with the row's code and marker; backup accepted.
|`CHK-FAILOVER-ADDR` |The device served the primary read before the backup read.
|`CHK-DECRYPT-ARM` |DECRYPT_OK appears once per decrypting slot (IV row primary plus an encrypted backup).
|`CHK-STIMULUS-SERVED` |The flash BFM served the primary's encryption_iv and encryption_kdf_input bytes as planted.
|===

*Anti-vacuity:* Asserts the primary payload is encrypted, the CLASS_KEY fuse relation to the packing key per row, and a DV AES model result (valid or broken PKCS\#7 pad) before boot; the encrypted backup's DECRYPT_OK is a positive control.

*Observation:* ROM console attempt markers (DECRYPT_\* outcome, MANIFEST_ERR=), DECRYPT_OK count, RSA marker counts, SPI served IV/KDF bytes, cold_scratch[0] verdict.

*Members:*

[cols="3,4,4,4,2",options="header"]
|===
|Test |Stimulus |Expected |Checkers it adds |Requirements
|`sep_firmware_invalid_decryption_class_key_test` |sep_efuse_lc_prod_bad_class_key.toml (CLASS_KEY = 0); image invalid_class_key.bin with encrypted primary and cleartext backup; packer seal kept. DV shortcut: +esrc_noise_force (ring-oscillator noise input forced; ESRC raw noise driven by esrc_noise_task) |primary verified, then DECRYPT_CLASS_KEY_EMPTY and OCA_FAIL_NO_PROVISIONED_SECRET (MANIFEST_ERR=0x0003001e); cleartext backup boots; DECRYPT_OK count 0. |None beyond the group |`SEP-ROM-PL-070`, `SEP-ROM-RETRY-030`
|`sep_firmware_invalid_encryption_iv_test` |Primary encryption_iv byte 0 XOR 0xFF (golden IV 0x10..0x1f), slot resealed; model asserts only plaintext byte 0 changes and no TOC rule breaks; class-key preload. DV shortcut: +esrc_noise_force (ring-oscillator noise input forced; ESRC raw noise driven by esrc_noise_task) |primary prints DECRYPT_OK then refused OCA_FAIL_PAYLOAD_HASH_CHAIN (MANIFEST_ERR=0x00030014); encrypted backup boots; DECRYPT_OK count 2. |None beyond the group |`SEP-ROM-PL-030`, `SEP-ROM-PL-060`, `SEP-ROM-RETRY-030`
|`sep_firmware_invalid_decryption_kdf_input_test` |Primary encryption_kdf_input byte 0 XOR 0xFF, slot resealed; model asserts the derived key differs and the pad is invalid; class-key preload. DV shortcut: +esrc_noise_force (ring-oscillator noise input forced; ESRC raw noise driven by esrc_noise_task) |primary prints AES_PAD_BAD and is refused OCA_FAIL_DECRYPT (MANIFEST_ERR=0x00030013); encrypted backup boots; DECRYPT_OK count 1. |None beyond the group |`SEP-ROM-PL-030`, `SEP-ROM-PL-060`, `SEP-ROM-RETRY-030`
|===

[[sep-rom-vplan-bl1-image]]
===== BL1 TOC-entry defects

*Tests:* 2.

*Base:* `hw/sys/sep/dv/cocotb/tests/rom_fw/sep_bl1_image_invalid_base.py`.

*Objective:* Proves that a BL1 TOC-entry size or entry-point defect planted in both re-signed slots is refused after MANIFEST_OK on each slot, before any BL1 copy or jump, and the boot ends terminal.

*Procedure:*

. Load sep_efuse_lc_prod.toml and assert LC raw 0x1 and SBOOT_DIS=0.
. Plant the member's BL1 entry defect in the primary and in the backup, re-hashing and re-signing each slot with dev0.
. Preload the OcahSpiFlash BFM, start esrc noise and console tasks, stage TCM via tcm_load_i, and boot from ROM over SPI.
. Poll cold_scratch[1] and the cold_scratch[0] verdict for up to 24M cycles, then watch 20k cycles for quiescence.
. Require primary then backup MANIFEST_SRC, no SBOOT_OFF, and MANIFEST_OK at least twice.
. Require the defect marker at least twice, once before and once after the backup read, the expected MANIFEST_ERR at least twice, MANIFEST_ALL_FAILED, and the mapped ERROR status word.
. Forbid other rejection markers and sibling BL1 arms, require fw_done with FAIL, and forbid all BL1 copy/jump markers.

*Checkers:*

[cols="2,5",options="header"]
|===
|Checker |Contract
|`CHK-STIMULUS-EFUSE` |PROD LC and SBOOT_DIS=0 are asserted before the eFuse image is written.
|`CHK-FAILOVER` |The primary MANIFEST_SRC precedes the backup MANIFEST_SRC.
|`CHK-CRYPTO-RAN` |SBOOT_OFF is absent and MANIFEST_OK appears at least twice.
|`CHK-BL1-DEFECT` |The defect marker appears at least twice, once before and once after the backup read.
|`CHK-REJECT-REASON` |The expected MANIFEST_ERR appears at least twice, MANIFEST_ALL_FAILED appears, and cold_scratch[1] held 0x0F01_0000\|status_for_result(code).
|`CHK-ONLY-REASON` |No other rejection marker or sibling BL1 arm marker appears.
|`CHK-TERMINAL` |fw_done is set and fw_pass is 0.
|`CHK-NO-HANDOFF` |None of BL1_TYPE=, COPY_SRC=, COPY_DST=, COPY_LEN=, BL1_COPIED, PRE_JUMP, BL1_JUMP= appears.
|`CHK-HANG` |After the verdict, status and console stay still for 20k cycles.
|===

*Anti-vacuity:* Asserts retired instructions, a non-empty console, both slot reads in order, and MANIFEST_OK at least twice with SBOOT_OFF absent, so the payload-stage check is what refused the slots.

*Observation:* Console markers (MANIFEST_SRC, MANIFEST_OK, defect marker, MANIFEST_ERR, MANIFEST_ALL_FAILED), cold_scratch[1] sequence, cold_scratch[0] verdict, and post-verdict quiescence.

*Members:*

[cols="3,4,4,4,2",options="header"]
|===
|Test |Stimulus |Expected |Checkers it adds |Requirements
|`sep_bl1_size_invalid_test` |Both slots: SEP_BL1 TOC entry length -> 0 (type and load_addr untouched), re-signed. DV shortcut: +esrc_noise_force (cocotb drives the entropy decorrelator inputs); tcm_load_i backdoor stages ROM .itcm/.dtcm hex with valid ECC before reset |Each slot refused OCA_BOOT_ERR_BASE\|OCA_FAIL_PAYLOAD_TOC after MANIFEST_OK; no BL1_SIZE/BL1_ADDR_RANGE/BL1_ENTRY_RANGE; MANIFEST_ALL_FAILED; terminal FAIL. |`CHK-STIMULUS-BL1-SIZE` |`SEP-ROM-MAN-060`, `SEP-ROM-RETRY-010`, `SEP-ROM-RETRY-030`, `SEP-ROM-RETRY-050`
|`sep_bl1_entry_invalid_test` |Both slots: SEP_BL1 entry_point -> length (boundary value), load_addr and length legal, re-signed. DV shortcut: +esrc_noise_force (cocotb drives the entropy decorrelator inputs); tcm_load_i backdoor stages ROM .itcm/.dtcm hex with valid ECC before reset |Each slot prints BL1_ENTRY_RANGE and MANIFEST_ERR=0x00030103 (OCA_BOOT_ERR_BL1_BAD_ADDR); status ERROR+SEP_MSG_MANIFEST_LOAD_FAILED (0x0F010213); MANIFEST_ALL_FAILED; terminal FAIL. |`CHK-STIMULUS-BL1-ENTRY` |`SEP-ROM-MAN-060`, `SEP-ROM-RETRY-010`, `SEP-ROM-RETRY-030`, `SEP-ROM-RETRY-050`
|===

*Added checker contracts:*

[cols="3,2,5",options="header"]
|===
|Test |Checker |Contract
|`sep_bl1_size_invalid_test` |`CHK-STIMULUS-BL1-SIZE` |The BL1 length was non-zero before and 0 after, and the entry type is still SEP_BL1.
|`sep_bl1_entry_invalid_test` |`CHK-STIMULUS-BL1-ENTRY` |The pre-mutation entry_point was below length and the written value equals length.
|===

==== Demotion

[[sep-rom-vplan-demotion-decision]]
===== Demotion decision table

*Tests:* 4.

*Base:* `hw/sys/sep/dv/cocotb/tests/rom_fw/sep_demotion_decision_base.py`.

*Objective:* Proves the [S25] demotion decision for one (LC, BL1 pair, BL2 pair) row: console tokens, DEMOTE_1/DEMOTE_2 register state, FEAT_CTRL and the boot-state PCR all match the expected outcome on a completed primary boot.

*Procedure:*

. Load the member's preload (PROD, PROD_END or PROD+sboot_dis); assert LC raw and SBOOT_DIS value.
. Verify both slots sealed, then plant demotion_control in the primary (in both slots for the no-flag row) and, for the sboot_dis row, clear secure_boot; narrow chiplet life_cycle_states and re-seal.
. Snapshot the planted selector_bits, demotion_control and secure_boot_control bytes and the primary manifest_hash.
. Start a per-cycle monitor of lcc_demote_state/lock_\{1,2} probes, esrc_noise_task and rom_console_task; serve the image from OcahSpiFlash.
. Release the core and poll cold_scratch[0] until BL1 reports PASS.
. Assert required/forbidden markers, single primary attempt and chain order MANIFEST_OK -> demotion tokens -> BL1_COPIED -> BL1_JUMP=.
. Compare DEMOTE registers, FEAT_CTRL and BL0S_BOOT_PCR against goldens; confirm the device served the planted bytes and no backup read.

*Checkers:*

[cols="2,5",options="header"]
|===
|Checker |Contract
|`CHK-VERDICT` |BL1 PASS verdict in cold_scratch[0]; SepBootScoreboard requires fw_pass and PC advance.
|`CHK-ROM-PATH` |Each required marker appears (BOOT_SPI, MANIFEST_SRC=0x1000, MANIFEST_OK, PAYLOAD_OK plus member markers).
|`CHK-BOOT-CHAIN-ORDER` |Single primary attempt and demotion tokens between MANIFEST_OK and BL1_COPIED.
|`CHK-DEMOTION-CONSOLE` |Each required demotion token exactly once after MANIFEST_OK, other rows' tokens absent.
|`CHK-DEMOTE-REGISTERS` |First probe sample is reset state, change count within bounds, final DEMOTE_1/DEMOTE_2 (demote, lock) equal the expected row.
|`CHK-DEMOTE-FEAT-CTRL` |lcc_feat_ctrl_probe_o equals feat_ctrl_expected(LC, SIP_DIS, SYS_DIS, DEMOTE_1, DEMOTE_2) with SEC_DIS=0.
|`CHK-MEAS-GOLDEN` |BL1-reported BL0S_BOOT_PCR equals SHA256(zero-PCR \|\| SHA256(record\{manifest_hash, lc, demotion bits, secure_boot, sboot_dis})).
|`CHK-NO-FAILOVER` |Primary magic served and no read touched the backup span.
|`CHK-STIMULUS-SERVED` |Device returned exactly the planted demotion/selector/secure_boot bytes.
|===

*Anti-vacuity:* Probe monitor must record the reset sample first and at least demote_changes_min samples in total (the reset sample included); SEC_DIS asserted 0 so the FEAT_CTRL compare depends on the DEMOTE state; stimulus read back offline and on the SPI bus.

*Observation:* ROM console demotion tokens, lcc_demote_state/lock_\{1,2}\_probe_o, lcc_feat_ctrl_probe_o, lcc_security_disable_probe_o, BL1 BL0S_BOOT_PCR print, SPI BFM transactions, cold_scratch[0] verdict.

*Members:*

[cols="3,4,4,4,2",options="header"]
|===
|Test |Stimulus |Expected |Checkers it adds |Requirements
|`sep_firmware_demotion_decision_auth_flag_0_prod_end_test` |Preload sep_efuse_lc_prod_end.toml (LC 0x8); primary demotion_control BL1_ENABLE=1, BL1_VALID=0, no BL2 pair; life_cycle_states PROD_END only; both slots re-sealed (signed). DV shortcut: +esrc_noise_force (forced ESRC noise input driven by esrc_noise_task). |boots on the signed primary; 'DEMOTE: PROD_END lock' and DEMOTE_LOCKED once; DEMOTE_1=(0,1), DEMOTE_2=(0,1); FEAT_CTRL and PCR golden match. |`CHK-STIMULUS-DEMOTION` |`SEP-ROM-DEM-020`, `SEP-ROM-DEM-050`
|`sep_firmware_demotion_decision_auth_flag_0_prod_sel_bit_set_test` |Preload sep_efuse_lc_prod_sboot_dis.toml (PROD, SBOOT_DIS=1); primary demotion_control VALID=1 ENABLE=1, no BL2; secure_boot cleared (NO_SIGNATURE); PROD-only life_cycle_states; backup re-sealed. |boots unsigned primary (FUSE: SBOOT_DIS: 1, SBOOT_OFF); BL1_DEMOTE=1, BL2_DEMOTE_DEC=0, DEMOTE_LOCKED; DEMOTE_1=(1,1), DEMOTE_2=(0,0); no RSA markers. |`CHK-STIMULUS-DEMOTION` |`SEP-ROM-DEM-030`, `SEP-ROM-DEM-040`, `SEP-ROM-DEM-050`, `SEP-ROM-SB-040`
|`sep_firmware_demotion_bl1_disable_secure_prod_test` |Preload sep_efuse_lc_prod.toml (PROD, SBOOT_DIS=0); primary demotion_control VALID=1 ENABLE=0, no BL2; signed RSA-3072; PROD-only life_cycle_states; both slots re-sealed. DV shortcut: +esrc_noise_force (forced ESRC noise input driven by esrc_noise_task). |boots signed primary (PUBK_AUTHORIZED, RSA_EXEC, RSA_VERIFY_OK); BL1_DEMOTE=0, BL2_DEMOTE_DEC=0, DEMOTE_LOCKED; DEMOTE_1=(0,1), DEMOTE_2=(0,0). |`CHK-STIMULUS-DEMOTION` |`SEP-ROM-DEM-010`, `SEP-ROM-DEM-030`, `SEP-ROM-DEM-040`, `SEP-ROM-DEM-050`
|`sep_firmware_demotion_decision_no_flag_prod_test` |Preload sep_efuse_lc_prod.toml; demotion_control cleared to 0 in both slots; signed RSA-3072 primary; PROD-only life_cycle_states; both re-sealed. DV shortcut: +esrc_noise_force (forced ESRC noise input driven by esrc_noise_task). |boots signed primary; 'DEMOTE: BL2 deferred, lock non-demoted', BL2_DEMOTE_DEC=0, DEMOTE_LOCKED; DEMOTE_1=(0,1), DEMOTE_2=(0,0). |`CHK-STIMULUS-DEMOTION` |`SEP-ROM-DEM-030`, `SEP-ROM-DEM-040`, `SEP-ROM-DEM-050`
|===

|`sep_firmware_demotion_decision_auth_flag_0_prod_sel_bit_set_test` |`CHK-STIMULUS-DEMOTION` |Log after asserting (VALID, ENABLE, BL2)=(1,1,0), secure_boot 0, NO_SIGNATURE, PROD-only life_cycle_states.
|`sep_firmware_demotion_bl1_disable_secure_prod_test` |`CHK-STIMULUS-DEMOTION` |Log after asserting (1,0,0), secure_boot enforced, RSA_3072, PROD-only life_cycle_states.
|`sep_firmware_demotion_decision_no_flag_prod_test` |`CHK-STIMULUS-DEMOTION` |Log after asserting demotion_control == 0, secure_boot enforced, RSA_3072.
|===

[[sep-rom-vplan-demotion-prod]]
===== Demotion under PROD

*Tests:* 6.

*Base:* `hw/sys/sep/dv/cocotb/tests/rom_fw/sep_demotion_prod_base.py`.

*Objective:* Proves the non-PROD_END [S25] demotion decision writes DEMOTE_1 (and never DEMOTE_2) per the BL1/BL2 demotion_control inputs of the booted primary manifest under PROD.

*Procedure:*

. Load preload sep_efuse_lc_prod_sboot_dis.toml (LC raw 0x1, SBOOT_DIS=1) and assert both values.
. Verify both shipped slots are sealed, then set primary demotion_control from the member's (\_SEL, \_AUTH, \_BL2) bits.
. Clear the primary secure_boot_control (signature_type NO_SIGNATURE) so the fuse disables secure boot; narrow both slots' chiplet lifecycle_states to PROD only and re-seal the backup.
. Start a DEMOTE_1/DEMOTE_2 state/lock probe monitor, serve the image over OcahSpiFlash (+sep_boot_from_spi) and boot to BL1 PASS on cold_scratch[0].
. Judge console: LC=PROD, FUSE: SBOOT_DIS: 1, SBOOT_OFF, the row's demotion tokens once each after MANIFEST_OK and before BL1_COPIED/BL1_JUMP=; other rows' tokens and RSA markers forbidden.
. Judge DEMOTE_1/DEMOTE_2 (demote, lock) probes and transition count, FEAT_CTRL against the lifecycle golden, and BL0S_BOOT_PCR against the Python measurement golden.
. Judge flash transactions: only the primary slot was read and it served the planted demotion/selector/secure_boot bytes.

*Checkers:*

[cols="2,5",options="header"]
|===
|Checker |Contract
|`CHK-STIMULUS-EFUSE` |LC raw and SBOOT_DIS equal the expected values.
|`CHK-STIMULUS-LC-CONSTRAINT` |Both slots' chiplet lifecycle_states equal PROD-only with the selector bit set and no package/system bits.
|`CHK-STIMULUS-DEMOTION` |Packed primary decodes to the member's (sel, auth, bl2), secure_boot off, NO_SIGNATURE, PROD-only LC, valid manifest hash.
|`CHK-BOOT-CHAIN-ORDER` |Only the primary attempt is accepted and MANIFEST_OK < last demotion token < BL1_COPIED < BL1_JUMP=.
|`CHK-DEMOTION-CONSOLE` |Required demotion tokens and values appear exactly once after MANIFEST_OK; all other demotion tokens absent.
|`CHK-DEMOTE-TRANSITIONS` |Every DEMOTE probe change is recorded with its cycle, and at least one sample is present.
|`CHK-DEMOTE-REGISTERS` |First sample is reset (0b10,0,0b10,0), change count within bounds, final DEMOTE_1/DEMOTE_2 (demote, lock) equal the expectation.
|`CHK-DEMOTE-FEAT-CTRL` |SEC_DIS clear and FEAT_CTRL equals feat_ctrl_expected(LC, SIP_DIS, SYS_DIS, DEMOTE_1, DEMOTE_2).
|`CHK-MEAS-GOLDEN` |BL0S_BOOT_PCR printed by BL1 equals SHA256(0\^32 \|\| SHA256(manifest_hash, lc, sboot, sboot_dis, demotion bits)).
|`CHK-NO-FAILOVER` |Primary manifest read returned OCAC magic and no read touched the backup span.
|`CHK-STIMULUS-SERVED` |Device served exactly the planted selector_bits, demotion_control and secure_boot_control bytes.
|===

*Anti-vacuity:* \__init_subclass__ cross-checks each member's declared outcome against outcome_for(); the stimulus is asserted offline and on the served SPI bytes, and the probe monitor must see the reset sample first.

*Observation:* Virt-console demotion tokens (BL1_DEMOTE=, BL2_DEMOTE_DEC=, DEMOTE_LOCKED/NOT_LOCKED, DEMOTE: BL2 deferred ...), lcc_demote_state/lock_\{1,2}\_probe_o, lcc_feat_ctrl_probe_o, lcc_security_disable_probe_o, BL1's BL0S_BOOT_PCR= line, OcahSpiFlash transactions, cold_scratch[0] verdict.

*Members:*

[cols="3,4,4,4,2",options="header"]
|===
|Test |Stimulus |Expected |Checkers it adds |Requirements
|`sep_firmware_demotion_decision_auth_flag_0_prod_test` |(sel, auth, bl2) = (0, 1, 0): demotion_control BL1_ENABLE=1, BL1_VALID=0, BL2 pair clear. Preload sep_efuse_lc_prod_sboot_dis.toml burns SBOOT_DIS so the unsigned primary boots under PROD |Boots; 'DEMOTE: BL2 deferred, lock non-demoted', BL2_DEMOTE_DEC=0, DEMOTE_LOCKED; DEMOTE_1=(0,1), DEMOTE_2=(0,0). |None beyond the group |`SEP-ROM-DEM-030`, `SEP-ROM-DEM-040`, `SEP-ROM-DEM-050`, `SEP-ROM-SB-040`
|`sep_firmware_demotion_decision_auth_flag_0_unauth_flag_0_prod_test` |(sel, auth, bl2) = (0, 1, 1): BL1_ENABLE=1, BL1_VALID=0, BL2_VALID=BL2_ENABLE=1. Preload sep_efuse_lc_prod_sboot_dis.toml burns SBOOT_DIS so the unsigned primary boots under PROD |Boots; 'DEMOTE: BL2 deferred, unlocked', BL2_DEMOTE_DEC=1, DEMOTE_NOT_LOCKED; DEMOTE_1=(0,0), DEMOTE_2=(0,0); exactly one probe sample for the run. |`CHK-DEMOTE-REGISTERS` |`SEP-ROM-DEM-030`, `SEP-ROM-DEM-040`, `SEP-ROM-DEM-050`, `SEP-ROM-SB-040`
|`sep_firmware_demotion_decision_no_flag_prod_sel_bit_set_test` |(sel, auth, bl2) = (1, 0, 0): BL1_VALID=1, BL1_ENABLE=0, BL2 pair clear. Preload sep_efuse_lc_prod_sboot_dis.toml burns SBOOT_DIS so the unsigned primary boots under PROD |Boots; BL1_DEMOTE=0, BL2_DEMOTE_DEC=0, DEMOTE_LOCKED; DEMOTE_1=(0,1), DEMOTE_2=(0,0). |None beyond the group |`SEP-ROM-DEM-030`, `SEP-ROM-DEM-040`, `SEP-ROM-DEM-050`, `SEP-ROM-SB-040`
|`sep_firmware_demotion_decision_auth_flag_0_unauth_flag_0_prod_sel_bit_set_test` |(sel, auth, bl2) = (1, 1, 1): BL1_VALID=BL1_ENABLE=1 and BL2_VALID=BL2_ENABLE=1. Preload sep_efuse_lc_prod_sboot_dis.toml burns SBOOT_DIS so the unsigned primary boots under PROD |Boots; BL1_DEMOTE=1, BL2_DEMOTE_DEC=1, DEMOTE_LOCKED; DEMOTE_1=(1,1), DEMOTE_2=(0,0). |None beyond the group |`SEP-ROM-DEM-030`, `SEP-ROM-DEM-040`, `SEP-ROM-DEM-050`, `SEP-ROM-SB-040`

Preload sep_efuse_lc_prod_sboot_dis.toml burns SBOOT_DIS so the unsigned primary boots under PROD |Boots; 'DEMOTE: BL2 deferred, unlocked', BL2_DEMOTE_DEC=1, DEMOTE_NOT_LOCKED; DEMOTE_1=(0,0), DEMOTE_2=(0,0); exactly one probe sample; boot PCR demotion bits 0x4. |`CHK-DEMOTE-REGISTERS` |`SEP-ROM-DEM-030`, `SEP-ROM-DEM-040`, `SEP-ROM-DEM-050`, `SEP-ROM-SB-040`

[[sep-rom-vplan-demotion-prod-end]]
===== Demotion under PROD_END

*Tests:* 4.

*Base:* `hw/sys/sep/dv/cocotb/tests/rom_fw/sep_demotion_prod_end_base.py`.

*Objective:* Proves that at PROD_END the ROM ignores every manifest demotion input and writes both DEMOTE_1 and DEMOTE_2 not demoted and locked, on a fully verified secure boot.

*Procedure:*

. Load preload sep_efuse_lc_prod_end.toml (LC raw 0x8, SBOOT_DIS=0) and assert both values.
. Verify both shipped slots are sealed, then set primary demotion_control from the member's (\_SEL, \_AUTH, \_BL2) bits.
. Narrow both slots' chiplet lifecycle_states to PROD_END only and re-seal (re-sign) both slots.
. Start the DEMOTE probe monitor and esrc noise, serve the image over OcahSpiFlash (+sep_boot_from_spi, +esrc_noise_force) and boot to BL1 PASS.
. Judge console: LC=PROD_END, PUBK_AUTHORIZED, RSA_EXEC, RSA_VERIFY_OK, MANIFEST_OK, 'DEMOTE: PROD_END lock' and DEMOTE_LOCKED once; BL1_DEMOTE=, BL2_DEMOTE_DEC= and SBOOT_OFF absent.
. Judge DEMOTE_1=(0,1) and DEMOTE_2=(0,1), FEAT_CTRL golden, and BL0S_BOOT_PCR with demotion bits 0x2.
. Judge flash transactions: only the primary was read and it served the planted demotion bytes.

*Checkers:*

[cols="2,5",options="header"]
|===
|Checker |Contract
|`CHK-STIMULUS-DEMOTION` |Packed primary decodes to the member's (sel, auth, bl2) and PROD_END-only lifecycle_states.
|`CHK-STIMULUS-LC-CONSTRAINT` |Both slots' lifecycle_states equal PROD_END-only with the selector bit set.
|`CHK-STIMULUS-EFUSE` |LC raw 0x8 and SBOOT_DIS=0 (inherited).
|`CHK-BOOT-CHAIN-ORDER` |Single primary attempt with PUBK_AUTHORIZED, RSA_EXEC, RSA_VERIFY_OK, MANIFEST_OK and PAYLOAD_OK in order, and MANIFEST_OK before the last demotion token, before BL1_COPIED, before BL1_JUMP= (inherited).
|`CHK-DEMOTION-CONSOLE` |'DEMOTE: PROD_END lock' and DEMOTE_LOCKED once after MANIFEST_OK; all other demotion tokens absent (inherited).
|`CHK-DEMOTE-REGISTERS` |Final DEMOTE_1=(0,1) and DEMOTE_2=(0,1) from reset (inherited).
|`CHK-DEMOTE-FEAT-CTRL` |FEAT_CTRL matches the non-demoted PROD_END profile (inherited).
|`CHK-MEAS-GOLDEN` |Boot PCR matches the golden with demotion bits 0x2 and bl2 forced 0 (inherited).
|`CHK-NO-FAILOVER` |No read touched the backup span (inherited).
|`CHK-STIMULUS-SERVED` |Device served the planted demotion inputs (inherited).
|===

*Anti-vacuity:* Planted inputs are asserted on the packed image and on the served SPI bytes, because the PROD_END consoles are identical across members; the DEMOTE_2 lock 0->1 proves the lock_2 probe is live.

*Observation:* Virt-console signed-path and demotion tokens, lcc_demote_state/lock_\{1,2}\_probe_o, lcc_feat_ctrl_probe_o, BL0S_BOOT_PCR=, OcahSpiFlash transactions, cold_scratch[0] verdict.

*Members:*

[cols="3,4,4,4,2",options="header"]
|===
|Test |Stimulus |Expected |Checkers it adds |Requirements
|`sep_firmware_demotion_decision_no_flag_prod_end_test` |(sel, auth, bl2) = (0, 0, 0). DV shortcut: +esrc_noise_force forces the ESRC noise input driven by esrc_noise_task |Boots; 'DEMOTE: PROD_END lock', DEMOTE_LOCKED; DEMOTE_1=(0,1), DEMOTE_2=(0,1). |None beyond the group |`SEP-ROM-DEM-020`, `SEP-ROM-DEM-050`
|`sep_firmware_demotion_decision_no_flag_prod_end_sel_bit_set_test` |(sel, auth, bl2) = (1, 0, 0): BL1_DEMOTION_VALID set. DV shortcut: +esrc_noise_force forces the ESRC noise input driven by esrc_noise_task |Boots; 'DEMOTE: PROD_END lock', DEMOTE_LOCKED, no BL1_DEMOTE=; DEMOTE_1=(0,1), DEMOTE_2=(0,1). |None beyond the group |`SEP-ROM-DEM-020`, `SEP-ROM-DEM-050`
|`sep_firmware_demotion_bl2_request_prod_end_test` |(sel, auth, bl2) = (0, 0, 1): BL2_VALID=BL2_ENABLE=1 in a re-signed primary. DV shortcut: +esrc_noise_force forces the ESRC noise input driven by esrc_noise_task |Boots; 'DEMOTE: PROD_END lock', no BL2_DEMOTE_DEC=; DEMOTE_1=(0,1), DEMOTE_2=(0,1); boot PCR bl2 bit 0. |None beyond the group |`SEP-ROM-DEM-020`, `SEP-ROM-DEM-050`
|`sep_firmware_demotion_decision_unauth_flag_30_prod_end_test` |(sel, auth, bl2) = (1, 1, 0): BL1_VALID=BL1_ENABLE=1 in a re-signed primary. DV shortcut: +esrc_noise_force forces the ESRC noise input driven by esrc_noise_task |Boots; 'DEMOTE: PROD_END lock', no BL1_DEMOTE=; DEMOTE_1=(0,1), DEMOTE_2=(0,1); FEAT_CTRL non-demoted; boot PCR without demotion. |None beyond the group |`SEP-ROM-DEM-020`, `SEP-ROM-DEM-050`
|===

==== Hardware stall timeouts

[[sep-rom-vplan-stall-timeout]]
===== Secure DMA and HMAC stalls

*Tests:* 2.

*Procedure:* each member is an independent test on the common bring-up. It applies the stimulus in its row, waits for the outcome in its row, and judges it with the checkers in its row.

*Members:*

[cols="3,4,4,4,2",options="header"]
|===
|Test |Stimulus |Expected |Checkers it adds |Requirements
|`sep_rom_dma_stall_terminal_test` |+sep_dma_host_stall holds the DMA host response channel idle; +sep_smc_mem_hex SMC-SRAM manifest path; TEST_DEV. DV shortcut: +sep_dma_host_stall (TB holds the DMA host port response idle). |terminal: DMA_TIMEOUT_STS= then MANIFEST_ERR=OCA_BOOT_ERR_DMA (0x00030100), WARN 0x08010229 before ERROR 0x0F010213, MANIFEST_ALL_FAILED, FAIL verdict. |`CHK-DMA-STALL-TIMEOUT`, `CHK-DMA-STALL-STATUS`, `CHK-DMA-STALL-TERMINAL`, `CHK-DMA-STALL-NO-BOOT` |`SEP-ROM-RETRY-050`
|`sep_rom_hmac_fifo_stall_terminal_test` |dut.hmac_fifo_drain_stall_i driven to 1 once CRYPTO_SELFTEST_OK appears on the console; TEST_DEV; no SPI flash. DV shortcut: Forced TB input hmac_fifo_drain_stall_i triggered from a console marker; tcm_load_i backdoor preload of boot_rom.itcm/dtcm.hex into ICCM/DCCM before reset. |terminal ROM_ERR_ROM_HASH_MISMATCH: SHA_FIFO_TIMEOUT then ROM_HASH_COMPUTE_FAIL, WARN 0x0801022A before 0x0F01F005, FAIL verdict, halt. |`CHK-HMAC-STALL-STIMULUS`, `CHK-HMAC-STALL-TIMEOUT`, `CHK-HMAC-STALL-STATUS`, `CHK-HMAC-STALL-TERMINAL`, `CHK-HMAC-STALL-NO-PROGRESS` |`SEP-ROM-ATT-060`, `SEP-ROM-RETRY-050`
|===

[[sep-rom-vplan-regression-groups]]
=== Regression Groups

[cols="1,2,3",options="header"]
|===
|Group |Tests |Purpose
|`rom_fw_smoke` |`sep_rom_non_secure_boot_test`, `sep_rom_ot_dma_boot_test`, `sep_manifest_magic_survives_handoff_test`, `sep_rom_ot_pio_boot_test`, `sep_rom_ot_secure_boot_test` |Bring-up of the SMC-SRAM, OpenTitan SPI DMA and PIO, and signed boot paths, and of the hand-off magic. Run it first after a ROM or toolchain change
|`rom_fw` |All 188 tests of `testlists/rom_fw.toml` |The full Boot ROM firmware regression and the credit set of this plan
|===

[source,bash]
----
python3 tools/dv/run_dv.py --dut sep --items rom_fw_smoke
python3 tools/dv/run_dv.py --dut sep --items rom_fw --regress \
  --sim-jobs 8 --build-jobs 24
----

The `c_compile` stage builds both firmware profiles. It needs a
picolibc-enabled RISC-V GCC or `scripts/docker-run.sh`, and the
`tt-oca-manifest` submodule:

[source,bash]
----
git submodule update --init hw/sys/sep/bootrom/prod/tools/tt-oca-manifest
----

Keep `--sim-jobs` near 8, because the threaded VeeR EL2 model can stall near
reset. Test timeouts are 1 to 6 hours of wall time.

[[sep-rom-vplan-known-limitations]]
=== Known Limitations

[cols="2,3",options="header"]
|===
|Limitation |Impact
|No CI tier runs `rom_fw` |Boot ROM evidence comes from a local run on a host with the toolchain
|Cold-path value of `cold_scratch[7]` |`rom.adoc` (Cold Boot Environment Setup, step 1, and Warm-Reset Resume Path, step 2) says the ROM clears the slot on a cold boot. `bl1-handoff.adoc` (Warm-reset return) says the ROM poisons it with `-1`, and `vector.S` writes `0xFFFFFFFF`. `sep_warm_reset_unarmed_cold_boot_test` expects `0xFFFFFFFF`. The two specification chapters disagree, and the ROM owner decides which value is correct
|Memory-repair gate |`SEP-ROM-SMC-030` names the `mem_repair_bypass` strap (`STRAPS_LO[13]`) as the escape. `vector.S` also continues on `STATUS_RPT` bit 2 (SKIP_MEM_CHECK), which `sep_efuse_map.rdl` lists as reserved, and also gates on the MBIST result. `sep_mbist_fail_continue_test` and `sep_firmware_mbist_only_fail_test` follow `vector.S` for these two behaviours
|===

[[sep-rom-vplan-formal]]
=== Formal Verification Plan

The Boot ROM has no formal flow.

[[sep-rom-vplan-checklist]]
=== Plan Checklist

[cols="4,1,4",options="header"]
|===
|Item |State |Note
|Every test in the summary has a procedure group |Yes |188 tests in 33 groups
|Every documented test exists in `rom_fw.toml` and in the `rom_fw` group |Yes |Checked against both
|Every test has at least one checker with a PASS record |Yes |Static check of the test source: 0 tests without one
|`rom_fw` has an `expected_count` |No |`rom_fw_smoke` has one. `rom_fw` does not, so a dropped entry does not fail the testlist load
|The ROM owner reviewed the requirement mappings |No |Pending owner review
|===
